			FATHER = story_owner
			MOTHER_ID = Moraqos_22
		}
		agot_canon_children_Adarys_3_start_valid_pregnancy_effect = yes
	}
	on_end = { }
	on_owner_death = { agot_canon_children_on_owner_death_effect = yes }
	effect_group = {
		months = agot_canon_children_pregnancies_cycle_months
		triggered_effect = {
			effect = {
				agot_canon_children_Adarys_3_start_valid_pregnancy_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

			FATHER = story_owner
			MOTHER_ID = Gamble_69
		}
		agot_canon_children_Combe_34_start_valid_pregnancy_effect = yes
	}
	on_end = { }
	on_owner_death = { agot_canon_children_on_owner_death_effect = yes }
	effect_group = {
		months = agot_canon_children_pregnancies_cycle_months
		triggered_effect = {
			effect = {
				agot_canon_children_Combe_34_start_valid_pregnancy_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

		}
		agot_canon_children_Cressey_20_start_valid_pregnancy_effect = yes
	}
	on_end = { }

	effect_group = {
		months = agot_canon_children_pregnancies_cycle_months
		triggered_effect = {
			effect = {
				agot_canon_children_Cressey_20_start_valid_pregnancy_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

		agot_canon_children_setup_father_effect = { FATHER = story_owner }
		agot_canon_children_setup_mother_effect = {
			FATHER = story_owner
			MOTHER_ID = Stark_104
		}
		agot_canon_children_Stark_100_start_valid_pregnancy_effect = yes
	}
	on_end = { }
	on_owner_death = { agot_canon_children_on_owner_death_effect = yes }
	effect_group = {
		months = agot_canon_children_pregnancies_cycle_months
		triggered_effect = {
			effect = {
				agot_canon_children_Stark_100_start_valid_pregnancy_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

		agot_canon_children_setup_father_effect = { FATHER = story_owner }
		agot_canon_children_setup_mother_effect = {
			FATHER = story_owner
			MOTHER_ID = Targaryen_137
		}
		agot_canon_children_Velaryon_11_start_valid_pregnancy_effect = yes
	}
	on_end = { }
	on_owner_death = { agot_canon_children_on_owner_death_effect = yes }
	effect_group = {
		months = agot_canon_children_pregnancies_cycle_months
		triggered_effect = {
			effect = {
				agot_canon_children_Velaryon_11_start_valid_pregnancy_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}
